\title{\textbf{The Renewal Programme Master Document}\\[0.35em]
\Large Predictive Store--Control Spacetime Emergence,\\[0.15em]
Source-Native ADM Closure and the One-Sequence Einstein Handoff,\\[0.15em]
the Universal Grand Tensor, and Arithmetic/Millennium Interfaces\\[0.3em]
\large Version V006}
\author{A.~P\'elissier}
\date{31 July 2026}

\begin{document}
\maketitle

\begin{abstract}
This version incorporates the dedicated spacetime-emergence programme through
V034 and the predictive renewal-cut programme through V022.  Relative to V005,
it adds four finite certificate packages: exact Store-frequency Hankel-pencil
tomography, depth-two semantic-clock closure and total-variance diagnostics,
coherent clock--geometry cross-source tomography, and quantitative stable
renewal-cut locality.  These additions replace abstract comparison clauses by
terminating moment, interference, Gram, and commutant tests.

The local entrance no longer assumes a pre-existing complex anchored microtick
or the fourth-order return identity as the flagship clock axiom.  It starts
from the predictive Radon--Nikodym envelope of one minimal Store channel, a
sharp recurrent real binary frame, and one switchable analytic Hamiltonian
Store germ.  Minimal Stinespring uniqueness makes the primitive automorphism
action on all canonical refinements natural.  A finite even Store-jet packet
reconstructs every loaded positive frequency, source weight, and annihilating
recurrence by an exact Hankel pencil.  Analytic cross-support then isolates the
loaded blocks, and finite Lie generation synthesizes the frequency-independent
polar quarter-root.  A real pointer--score pulse is conjugated by that derived
polar control to the complex phase-clock pulse.  The old anchored-return theorem
is retained only as an economical sufficient realization, not as the top-level
entrance.

The spatial source and geometrodynamic results are strengthened rather than
renamed.  The twenty-four-outcome metric instrument has coherent support
$\mathbf1_+\oplus\mathbf3_-$, a unique native determinant line, and normalized
exterior coefficient $1/27$.  The determinant response gives the DeWitt form
$\Tr(\Pi^2)-\tfrac12(\Tr\Pi)^2$ independently of future-cyclic multiplicity.
The score instrument is first audited by a persistent-transfer test, an exact
frequency-mixing commutator, mixed Store--score moments, and a depth-two
criterion equivalent to an all-depth common source clock.  Its fourth-order
failure splits exactly into intra-frequency semantic multiplicity and
inter-frequency central loading variance.

Equality of marginal clock and curvature Hessians remains insufficient for a
common action.  Whenever coherent comparison is physical, four phase-controlled
branch preparations reconstruct the complete cross Gram of the actual semantic
clock source $S_{\rm clk}$ and determinant/curvature source $S_{\rm geo}$.
The resulting source Schur complement, sector amplitudes and phases,
cross-Hankel residuals, and chronology degree give a terminating alternative.
Exact source, amplitude, phase, chronology, and persistent-score equality yields
$S_{\rm clk}=S_{\rm geo}$ and hence one physical stiffness, $c=1/2$, and
$ab=1$.  If no amplitude-level bridge is admitted, physical same-history
identity is explicitly nonidentifiable rather than inferred from marginal data.

The complete-cut freshness hypothesis is no longer used to infer a
Levi--Civita connection.  A rank-one complete cut gives a canonical past--fresh
split; exact naturality on an irreducible loaded past sector gives product
factorization, while approximate naturality gives an explicit operator and
kernel error bound.  A common rank-one fresh factor is also proved unable to
create or erase a clock--geometry source short.  Primitive two-cell link
tomography, Clifford multiplicativity, polar stretch, orientation holonomy,
discrete torsion, and same-history plaquette composition independently give the
Levi--Civita / Riemann--Cartan / metric-affine / no-connection alternative.
The finite normal commutator admits an exact five-term anomaly identity
separating source, normal-structure, connection, nuisance-interface, and scaling
defects.  A bounded heat-bath future can establish energy-damped screen
exhaustion but, under ordinary scaling, cannot serve as the gravitational
normal constraint.

The continuum statement is consequently reformulated as a one-regulator,
one-sequence variational theorem.  The four clauses formerly bundled into
ESSC---screen exhaustion, interacting scaling, $C^1_{\rm loc}$ generating-
functional convergence, and Ward closure---are independent and separately
certified.  An explicit moving-frame scalar Store has an exact matter
self-bracket and universal relative coupling, while the canonical-to-covariant
Legendre transform yields
\[
  2\chi\bigl(G_{\mu\nu}+\Lambda_{\rm cov}g_{\mu\nu}\bigr)=T_{\mu\nu},
  \qquad \nabla^\mu T_{\mu\nu}=0,
  \qquad \Lambda_{\rm cov}=\frac{\Lambda_H}{2\chi},
\]
only after the common connection, deformation, compactness, concentration,
matter, and first-variation certificates vanish on the same regulator
sequence.  The effective Newton scale is metrological,
$G_{\rm eff}=\ell_*^2/(16\pi\chi)$; neither $G$ nor $\Lambda$ follows from the
relative ADM normalization.  Orientation selection is also separated from
orientation existence by explicit Dobrushin no-go, Peierls/boundary-selection,
and superselection alternatives.  Arithmetic, gauge, PDE, algebraic, and
complexity ports retain their sector-specific open coercive estimates.
\end{abstract}

\section{Executive master statement and proof-status discipline}
\label{sec:executive-master}
% =============================================================================

\begin{definition}[Four proof-status labels]
Every claim in this master document is assigned one of four logical statuses.
\begin{enumerate}[label=\textup{(\roman*)},leftmargin=2.7em]
\item \emph{proved here}: a complete finite-dimensional, analytic, or
      operator-theoretic proof is included in this file;
\item \emph{established module}: the result is imported from the cumulative
      renewal notes, with its hypotheses and exact logical role restated;
\item \emph{conditional bridge}: the implication is proved once an explicitly
      named sector certificate is supplied;
\item \emph{open certificate}: the stated certificate is not proved by the
      common architecture and contains the remaining problem-specific content.
\end{enumerate}
A selector identity is not promoted to a solution theorem unless its loading,
coercivity, support, continuum, or resource certificate has also been proved.
\end{definition}

\begin{assessment}[Current strongest defensible headline]
The programme now has a strictly layered theorem rather than one long
conditional chain.  Finite Store jets reconstruct the loaded frequency packet,
and a predictive Store--control certificate derives the polar clock and
external Lorentzian Clifford cell.  One- and two-depth score experiments decide
the all-depth semantic clock after frequency mixing is excluded.  A physical
metric source and determinant response derive the spatial metric, volume, and
DeWitt coefficient.  Coherent cross-source tomography and the joint
clock--geometry Gram decide, rather than assume, whether the kinetic and
curvature branches are one same-history action.  A physical link/face audit
decides the connection branch, and an exact finite anomaly identity decides
deformation closure.  Matter and the covariant Einstein equation enter only
after a common-regulator compactness and first-variation pass.  Orientation
selection and the numerical values of $G$ and $\Lambda$ remain separate phase
and metrological questions.
\end{assessment}

\begin{theorem}[Strengthened Layered Renewal Programme Master Theorem]
\label{thm:layered-programme-master}
Let a bare finite predictive-renewal datum satisfy the predictive
Store--control entrance of \Cref{ass:predictive-clock-entry}.  Whenever metric
response is invoked, assume the persistent score-transfer and source-loading
parts of that entrance.  Then the following statements hold, with the further
hypotheses stated in the corresponding items.
\begin{enumerate}[label=\textup{(\Roman*)},leftmargin=3em]
\item The predictive refinement interval of the primitive Store is canonically
      the effect interval of its unique minimal Stinespring environment.
      Primitive process automorphisms lift uniquely and transport every
      canonical refinement naturally.  On the pointer source-cyclic carrier, a
      terminating Hankel-rank audit of the even Store jets reconstructs every
      loaded positive frequency, its source weight, and the exact annihilating
      recurrence without a sharp central-frequency Read.  On the positive
      Store-active support, one analytic Hamiltonian Store germ with blockwise
      cross-support generates the full blockwise $\mathrm{PU}(2)$ control group
      and hence the canonical frequency-independent polar quarter-root.  A real
      pointer--score pulse is thereby converted into the polar score clock.  A
      zero Store-frequency block, if present, remains an explicit static rebit
      countersector.
\item Two locally addressable positive-support clock copies generate a marked
      external
      \[
        M_4(\C)\cong\Cl_{1,3}(\C)
      \]
      factor and a physical six-direction Read.  Complete future separation is
      consumed only on the loaded odd/order lines.  Full environment-POVM
      saturation, an independent PAN axiom, frequency primitivity, and a sharp
      central-frequency PVM are not used.
\item The twenty-four-outcome metric instrument has coherent one-Read support
      $\mathbf1_+\oplus\mathbf3_-$ and native determinant coefficient $1/27$.
      A nondegenerate grade-one writer gives a positive spatial metric and the
      same Clifford/exterior volume line.  Every fixed positive response loaded
      by that volume source is a scalar multiple of $\det q$; its normalized
      Hessian has signature $(5,1)$ and cotangent inverse
      \[
        \Tr(\Pi^2)-\frac12(\Tr\Pi)^2.
      \]
      Higher source-cyclic rank changes only the overall stiffness when all
      modes share the same metric-volume loading.
\item The semantic score family is first decided by a persistent-transfer
      test, a frequency-mixing commutator, mixed Store--score moments, and an
      exact one- and two-depth common-clock criterion.  Let $S_{\rm clk}$ and
      $S_{\rm geo}$ be the resulting semantic-clock and determinant/curvature
      source maps in one null-completed quotient.  When coherent branch
      comparison is physical, four phase-controlled preparations reconstruct
      their cross Gram and reduce every same-range discrepancy to two amplitudes
      and two phases on $\mathbf1\oplus V_5$.  Otherwise physical source identity
      is nonidentifiable.  The joint Gram Schur complement and cross-Hankel
      residual then give a terminating alternative between an extra source, a
      positive chronology filter, an amplitude/phase split, a marked semantic
      loading, and exact physical source identity.  Only in the final case does
      one obtain
      \[
        \chi_{\rm clk}=\chi_{\rm geom},\qquad
        c=\frac12,\qquad
        a=\chi_{\rm geom}^{-1},\qquad
        b=\chi_{\rm geom},\qquad ab=1.
      \]
      Equality of marginal Hessians or of all marginal moments alone is
      insufficient.
\item Primitive two-cell link tomography yields exactly one of four connection
      branches: Levi--Civita, metric-compatible torsion/Riemann--Cartan,
      metric-affine nonmetricity, or failure of frame closure.  Same-history
      plaquette composition is an independent finite test.  On the
      Levi--Civita branch, the finite normal commutator differs from the target
      hypersurface-deformation bracket by the exact sum of source, normal,
      connection, nuisance-interface, and scaling anomalies.
\item The endpoint-local moving-frame scalar Store has an exact matter
      self-bracket with the same metric structure function.  Positivity of the
      species-current Gram forces every positive relative matter amplitude to
      one.  A gravity--matter unitary action orbit is stronger than necessary:
      universal relative coupling and the covariant Einstein--matter equations
      are compatible with a maximal typed-sector branch contrast.
\item If, on one regulator sequence, the physical connection, face,
      deformation, matter, source-completeness, compactness, concentration,
      Legendre, boundary, and first-variation defects all vanish, then a
      subsequence converges to
      \[
        2\chi\bigl(G_{\mu\nu}+\Lambda_{\rm cov}g_{\mu\nu}\bigr)
        =T_{\mu\nu},
        \qquad
        \nabla^\mu T_{\mu\nu}=0,
        \qquad
        \Lambda_{\rm cov}=\frac{\Lambda_H}{2\chi}.
      \]
      The four continuum clauses---exhaustion, interacting scaling,
      $C^1_{\rm loc}$ renormalization, and Ward closure---are independent and
      must be verified on that same sequence.  A heat-bath future may establish
      exhaustion but does not thereby become the gravitational normal
      constraint.
\item The primitive deck interaction gives a finite phase alternative.  A
      uniform Dobrushin margin implies one deck-even phase and forbids
      spontaneous or boundary orientation selection.  A sufficiently strong
      ferromagnetic primitive edge ratio gives distinct boundary-selected
      phases, and the zero-temperature positive-coupling limit gives two
      disjoint factorial sectors.  Equilibrium orientation order still has zero
      entropy production and is not a thermodynamic arrow of time.
\item The overall stiffness and homogeneous vacuum source are independent
      metrological data.  A scalar spin-two response yields
      \[
        G_{\rm eff}=\frac{\ell_*^2}{16\pi\chi},
      \]
      while an absolute homogeneous-volume score yields $\Lambda_H$.
      Normalized Store--Read probabilities are exactly blind to an arbitrary
      overall vacuum loading.
\item At every cutoff, the growing Grand Tensor retains its exact
      common-source, Hankel-saturation, and sector-selector structure.  The
      arithmetic, Yang--Mills, fluid, algebraic, and complexity interfaces
      remain conditional on their displayed sector-specific coercive or
      comparison estimates; none is promoted to a solution theorem by the
      spacetime results.
\end{enumerate}
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Items \textup{(I)--(II)} are
\Cref{thm:predictive-RN-master,thm:store-autocorrelation-master,thm:store-frequency-pencil-master,cor:store-frequency-count-master,thm:analytic-store-polar-master,thm:predictive-clock-discharge,thm:anchor-external-clifford-master}.
Item \textup{(III)} is
\Cref{thm:flagship-kraus-source-master,cor:native-determinant-master,thm:single-writer-clifford-master,thm:exterior-cube-master,thm:spectral-source-Ward-master}.
Item \textup{(IV)} is
\Cref{prop:score-effect-depth-no-go-master,thm:score-frequency-mixing-master,thm:mixed-score-reconstruction-master,thm:semantic-clock-depth-two-master,thm:semantic-total-variance-master,thm:cross-source-tomography-master,thm:ADM-action-audit-master,thm:clock-geometry-cross-Hankel-master,thm:Clifford-common-action-master}.
Item \textup{(V)} is
\Cref{thm:physical-connection-master,thm:deformation-anomaly-master}.
Item \textup{(VI)} is \Cref{thm:moving-frame-matter-master}.
Item \textup{(VII)} is \Cref{thm:Einstein-extension-master}.
Item \textup{(VIII)} is \Cref{thm:orientation-phase-master}.
Item \textup{(IX)} is \Cref{thm:metrology-master}.  Item \textup{(X)} is
\Cref{thm:physical-source-completeness,thm:primitive-sector-saturation,thm:character-leakage-master,cth:ambient-source-master,thm:source-regulator-master}
and the sector theorems in \Cref{sec:millennium-interface}.
\end{proof}

\section{The predictive Store--control entrance to spacetime}
\label{sec:predictive-store-control-entry}
\label{sec:predictive-clock-entry}
\label{sec:two-principle-entry}
% =============================================================================

\begin{definition}[Bare finite predictive-renewal datum]
\label{def:bare-renewal}
A bare datum consists of a finite-dimensional convex operational process
system with a unique deterministic discard on every object, reduction by
complete contextual future equivalence, closure under finite sequential and
parallel composition, classical control and coarse graining, and a designated
strongly recurrent component whenever regeneration is claimed.  No manifold,
metric, causal cone, time orientation, Clifford algebra, spatial rank, or
continuum topology is included in the datum.
\end{definition}

\begin{assumption}[Predictive Store--control entrance]
\label{ass:predictive-store-control-entry}
\label{ass:predictive-clock-entry}
Select one strongly recurrent component and assume the following finite,
operationally checkable data.
\begin{enumerate}[label=\textup{(S\arabic*)},leftmargin=3em]
\item The primitive Store channel has a finite minimal Stinespring realization.
      The admitted refinement family contains every CP submap actually used
      below and separates the loaded source-cyclic lines.  Full arbitrary
      environment-POVM access is not assumed.
\item The primitive automorphism group is computed from the unmarked relational
      data---types, composition, probabilities, discard, depth, cost, and
      future incidence---before any metric, Clifford generator, orientation, or
      continuum coordinate is introduced.  The Store channel and admitted
      future class are invariant under this group.
\item The recurrent active carrier contains a sharp real binary frame $(X,Z)$
      and a norm-continuous reflection-reversed Hamiltonian Store tone with
      centered skew generator $L$ satisfying
      \[
        [L,X]=0,\qquad ZLZ=-L,\qquad LZ\ne0.
      \]
      The positive Store-frequency support is retained; any zero-frequency
      block is separately marked rather than silently complexified.
\item There is one switchable real-analytic Hamiltonian control germ whose
      coefficient algebra is $M_2(\C)$ on every loaded positive Store-frequency
      block.  Positive and negative dwell times, finite concatenation, and
      finite classical control are admitted.  This clause is a reversible
      control assumption, not a consequence of effect/refinement saturation.
\item The loaded Store-order contrast is future consequential.  For
      geometrodynamic response, there is additionally one physical real
      pointer--score pulse and a controlled score instrument.  Its one- and
      two-depth successes, the finite mixed Store--score histories used below,
      absolute loading, and coherent clock--geometry branch comparisons are
      retained whenever Newton, vacuum, or common-action coefficients are
      claimed.  Whenever powers of one positive score transfer are used, the
      measured instrument must pass the persistent-transfer certificate of
      \Cref{def:persistent-score-transfer-master}.
\item Two positive-support clock copies are locally addressable.  Their local
      pulses, sharp binary Reads, swaps, finite randomization, parity coarse
      graining, and complete external replacement channels are admitted.  No
      tensor power beyond depth two is used by the finite Lorentzian cell.
\item The selected renewal graph is finite, strongly connected, not a single
      directed cycle, and has positive finite depths and finite local barriers.
\end{enumerate}
\end{assumption}

\begin{assumption}[Optional complete renewal-cut freshness entrance]
\label{ass:complete-cut-entry}
This assumption is used only when an exact fresh replacement after a complete
cut is required.  At one future-accessible one-tick-versus-rest cut:
\begin{enumerate}[label=\textup{(F\arabic*)},leftmargin=3em]
\item the complete reset response matrix has rank one;
\item its minimal physical dilation is retained;
\item every surviving past-memory isotypic or multiplicity label is retained,
      and an unconditional product claim is restricted to a selected loaded
      past sector on which the past-record action is complex irreducible;
\item the post-cut controls are natural under that unmarked past action and the
      clock slots are related by a transitive physical permutation action.
      Reducible sectors are treated only after their consequential commutant
      memory is explicitly retained or physically resolved.
\end{enumerate}
This certificate derives a fresh replacement factor.  It is neither necessary
nor sufficient for Levi--Civita transport: metricity, torsion, and plaquette
composition are decided independently by \Cref{thm:physical-connection-master}.
\end{assumption}

\subsection{Predictive Radon--Nikodym naturality and consequential accessibility}


\begin{theorem}[Predictive Radon--Nikodym envelope]
\label{thm:predictive-RN-master}
Let $(V,\mathcal E)$ be a minimal Stinespring realization of a finite UCP map
$\Phi:\mathcal B(\K)\to\mathcal B(\Hh)$.  For $F\in\mathcal B(\mathcal E)$ put
\[
  \Theta_V(F)(X)=V^*(X\otimes F)V.
\]
Then
\[
 \boxed{
 \{F:0\preceq F\preceq I_{\mathcal E}\}
 \longrightarrow
 \{\Psi:0\le_{\rm CP}\Psi\le_{\rm CP}\Phi\},
 \qquad F\longmapsto\Theta_V(F)}
\]
is an affine order isomorphism.  Finite POVMs on $\mathcal E$ correspond
bijectively to finite CP decompositions of $\Phi$.  Every physical channel
automorphism lifts uniquely to $\mathcal E$ and acts on this order interval by
$F\mapsto W_gFW_g^*$.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Positivity of $F$ makes $\Theta_V(F)$ completely positive, and
$0\preceq F\preceq I$ gives
$0\le_{\rm CP}\Theta_V(F)\le_{\rm CP}\Phi$.  Conversely, for
$0\le_{\rm CP}\Psi\le_{\rm CP}\Phi$, the finite Radon--Nikodym theorem for CP
maps gives a positive contraction $F$ in the commutant of the represented
system algebra; minimality identifies that commutant with
$I\otimes\mathcal B(\mathcal E)$, giving the displayed form.  If
$\Theta_V(F)=0$, then
$\langle (X\otimes I)V\xi,(I\otimes F)(Y\otimes I)V\eta\rangle=0$
for all $X,Y,\xi,\eta$.  The Stinespring cyclic span is dense, so $F=0$;
therefore the coordinate is unique and the map is injective.  The POVM claim
follows by summing the coordinates.  The automorphism statement is the
minimal-Stinespring uniqueness argument of
\Cref{thm:minimal-realization-naturality-master}.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Minimal-realization naturality]
\label{thm:minimal-realization-naturality-master}
Let $\Phi:\mathcal B(\K)\to\mathcal B(\Hh)$ be a finite channel with minimal
Stinespring isometry
\[
 V:\Hh\longrightarrow\K\otimes\mathcal E.
\]
Suppose a physical automorphism $g$ has unitary implementations $U_g$ and
$Z_g$ satisfying
\[
 U_g^*\Phi(X)U_g=\Phi(Z_g^*XZ_g).
\]
Then there is a unique unitary $W_g$ on $\mathcal E$ such that
\[
 \boxed{(Z_g\otimes W_g)V=VU_g.}
\]
If $U_gU_h=a(g,h)U_{gh}$ and $Z_gZ_h=b(g,h)Z_{gh}$, then
\[
 \boxed{W_gW_h=a(g,h)b(g,h)^{-1}W_{gh}.}
\]
For every Radon--Nikodym refinement
\[
 \Theta_V(F)(X)=V^*(X\otimes F)V
\]
one has
\[
 \boxed{g\cdot\Theta_V(F)=\Theta_V(W_gFW_g^*).}
\]
Consequently the whole admitted refinement family and its generated record
algebra are natural.  Any Read or reset uniquely characterized by invariant
physical data is natural, up to the induced outcome permutation.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
The two isometries $VU_g$ and $(Z_g\otimes I)V$ dilate the same channel, since
\[
 (VU_g)^*(X\otimes I)(VU_g)=U_g^*\Phi(X)U_g
 =\Phi(Z_g^*XZ_g).
\]
Both are minimal: multiplication by a unitary on either represented factor
preserves the Stinespring cyclic span.  Minimal Stinespring uniqueness gives a
unique $W_g$ satisfying the displayed intertwining equation.  Applying the
equation successively to $g$ and $h$ and using uniqueness gives the cocycle
law.  Finally,
\[
\begin{aligned}
 (g\cdot\Theta_V(F))(X)
 &=U_gV^*(Z_g^*XZ_g\otimes F)VU_g^*\\
 &=V^*(X\otimes W_gFW_g^*)V.
\end{aligned}
\]
Conjugation preserves effects and POVMs.  If an invariantly defined class has a
unique Read or reset, its conjugate belongs to the same class and hence equals
the original object, with only the unique outcome relabeling allowed.  This is
exactly naturality; an arbitrary hand-selected effect need not be pointwise
fixed.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Consequential odd-line Read extraction]
\label{thm:consequential-odd-read-master}
Let $X$ be a finite reduced predictive order-unit space, let $s$ be a physical
involution, and let $0\ne d\in X$ satisfy $sd=-d$ and $u(d)=0$.  Assume the
admitted effect body is invariant under $s^*$, complement, and finite convex
mixing.  Then complete future separation supplies an admitted effect $e$ with
$e(d)\ne0$, and
\[
 \boxed{
 e_+=\frac12(e+u-s^*e),
 \qquad e_-=u-e_+
 }
\]
is an admitted binary Read satisfying
\[
 s^*e_+=e_-,
 \qquad
 e_+(d)=-e_-(d)\ne0.
\]
The two outcomes are future distinct, and $s$ acts by $-1$ on their real
contrast line.  Every recurrent loop implementing $s$ therefore has
determinant holonomy $-1$ on that line.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Because $d$ is nonzero in the reduced future quotient, some admitted effect
separates it.  Both $e$ and $u-s^*e$ are effects, so their average $e_+$ is an
admitted effect; complement closure gives $e_-$.  Directly,
\[
 s^*e_+=\frac12(s^*e+u-e)=u-e_+=e_-.
\]
Using $u(d)=0$ and $sd=-d$,
\[
 e_+(d)=\frac12\bigl(e(d)-s^*e(d)\bigr)
       =\frac12\bigl(e(d)-e(sd)\bigr)=e(d)\ne0.
\]
Thus the two labels survive the future quotient.  Exchange fixes their
normalization vector and negates their contrast vector, proving the determinant
statement.
\end{proof}

\begin{firewall}[Why full environment completeness is obsolete]
The preceding theorem consumes one separating future on one loaded odd line.
It does not require every mathematically possible effect or POVM on a minimal
Stinespring environment.  Minimality alone is still insufficient: a minimal
dilation equipped only with scalar admitted environment effects can retain two
distinct environment states that no physical future distinguishes.  The
irreducible residual premise is therefore future separation of the distinctions
actually used, not universal environment access.
\end{firewall}


\subsection{Finite Store-frequency tomography from Store jets}
\label{sec:store-frequency-tomography-master}

Equip the finite pointer source-cyclic operator carrier with its retained
Hilbert--Schmidt inner product.  Let $\delta=\operatorname{ad}_L$ be the
skew-adjoint Store derivation generated by the retained real pointer $Z$.  On its loaded positive support put
\[
  \mathscr K:=-\frac14\delta^2
  =\sum_{j=1}^{s}\lambda_je_j,
  \qquad
  0<\lambda_1<\cdots<\lambda_s,
  \qquad
  z_j=e_jZ,
\]
where only the nonzero $z_j$ are retained.  Write
\[
  w_j=\norm{z_j}^2>0,
  \qquad
  \mu_j=\sqrt{\lambda_j}.
\]
The physical pointer oscillation frequency on the $j$th loaded plane is
$2\mu_j$, while $\mu_j$ is the positive polar-dwell rate used below.

\begin{definition}[Store-frequency moment packet]
\label{def:store-frequency-moments-master}
For $n\ge0$ define
\begin{equation}
  \boxed{
  m_n:=\langle Z,\mathscr K^nZ\rangle
      =\sum_{j=1}^{s}w_j\lambda_j^n.}
  \label{eq:store-frequency-moments-master}
\end{equation}
\end{definition}

\begin{theorem}[Store-jet/autocorrelation identity]
\label{thm:store-autocorrelation-master}
For
\[
  c_Z(t):=\langle Z,e^{t\delta}Z\rangle
\]
one has
\begin{equation}
  \boxed{c_Z(t)=\sum_{j=1}^{s}w_j\cos(2\mu_jt).}
  \label{eq:store-autocorrelation-master}
\end{equation}
Consequently every odd derivative vanishes at the origin and
\begin{equation}
  \boxed{m_n=\frac{(-1)^n}{4^n}c_Z^{(2n)}(0).}
  \label{eq:store-jet-moments-master}
\end{equation}
Thus one finite even Store-jet packet, or the corresponding derivatives of one
physical order-autocorrelation experiment, determines the moment data used by
the spectral reconstruction.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
The spectral subspaces of $\mathscr K$ reduce $\delta$, and
$\delta^2z_j=-4\lambda_jz_j$.  Skew-adjointness gives
$\langle z_j,\delta z_j\rangle=0$, so the power series of $e^{t\delta}$ yields
\[
  \langle z_j,e^{t\delta}z_j\rangle
  =w_j\cos(2\sqrt{\lambda_j}t).
\]
Distinct spectral subspaces are orthogonal.  Summation proves
\eqref{eq:store-autocorrelation-master}, and differentiation gives
$c_Z^{(2n)}(0)=(-4)^nm_n$.
\end{proof}

For $r\ge1$ define
\begin{align}
  H_0^{(r)}&:=\bigl[m_{a+b}\bigr]_{a,b=0}^{r-1},
  \label{eq:store-H0-master}\\
  H_1^{(r)}&:=\bigl[m_{a+b+1}\bigr]_{a,b=0}^{r-1}.
  \label{eq:store-H1-master}
\end{align}

\begin{theorem}[Exact finite Store-frequency pencil]
\label{thm:store-frequency-pencil-master}
Let $s$ be the number of distinct loaded positive values above.  Then:
\begin{enumerate}[label=\textup{(\roman*)},leftmargin=2.8em]
\item $H_0^{(s)}\succ0$ and $H_1^{(s)}\succeq0$;
\item the generalized eigenvalues of
      \begin{equation}
        H_1^{(s)}x=\lambda H_0^{(s)}x
        \label{eq:store-pencil-eigen-master}
      \end{equation}
      are exactly $\lambda_1,\ldots,\lambda_s$, each once;
\item if
      \[
        q(t)=t^s+q_{s-1}t^{s-1}+\cdots+q_0
            =\prod_{j=1}^{s}(t-\lambda_j),
      \]
      then
      \begin{equation}
        \boxed{
        H_0^{(s)}
        \begin{pmatrix}q_0\\ \vdots\\ q_{s-1}\end{pmatrix}
        =-
        \begin{pmatrix}m_s\\ \vdots\\ m_{2s-1}\end{pmatrix};}
        \label{eq:store-annihilator-master}
      \end{equation}
\item every later moment satisfies
      \begin{equation}
        \boxed{
        m_{n+s}+\sum_{k=0}^{s-1}q_km_{n+k}=0
        \qquad(n\ge0);}
        \label{eq:store-recurrence-master}
      \end{equation}
\item with $V_{aj}=\lambda_j^a$, $0\le a\le s-1$,
      \begin{equation}
        \boxed{
        \begin{pmatrix}w_1\\ \vdots\\ w_s\end{pmatrix}
        =V^{-1}
        \begin{pmatrix}m_0\\ \vdots\\ m_{s-1}\end{pmatrix}.}
        \label{eq:store-weight-recovery-master}
      \end{equation}
\end{enumerate}
Hence the first $2s$ Store moments determine the complete loaded positive
Store spectrum, its pointer weights, and its exact finite recurrence.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Let
\[
  V_{aj}=\lambda_j^a,
  \qquad
  W=\diag(w_1,\ldots,w_s),
  \qquad
  \Lambda=\diag(\lambda_1,\ldots,\lambda_s).
\]
Then
\[
  H_0^{(s)}=VWV^{\mathsf T},
  \qquad
  H_1^{(s)}=VW\Lambda V^{\mathsf T}.
\]
The Vandermonde matrix $V$ is invertible and $W\succ0$, proving the first
claim.  For $x_j=V^{-\mathsf T}e_j$,
\[
  H_1^{(s)}x_j=VW\Lambda e_j
  =\lambda_jVWe_j=\lambda_jH_0^{(s)}x_j,
\]
and the $x_j$ form a basis.  Since $q(\lambda_j)=0$,
\[
  0=\sum_jw_j\lambda_j^nq(\lambda_j)
   =m_{n+s}+\sum_{k=0}^{s-1}q_km_{n+k}.
\]
Taking $0\le n\le s-1$ gives the linear system and its uniqueness; arbitrary
$n$ gives the recurrence.  The first $s$ moment equations are $Vw=m$, proving
the weight formula.
\end{proof}

\begin{corollary}[Exact loaded-frequency count and source reconstruction]
\label{cor:store-frequency-count-master}
For $\mathbb H_r=[m_{a+b}]_{a,b=0}^{r}$,
\begin{equation}
  \boxed{\rank\mathbb H_r=\min\{r+1,s\}.}
  \label{eq:store-Hankel-rank-master}
\end{equation}
Thus the first vanishing scalar Hankel shell determines $s$ without knowing it
in advance.  After the values $\lambda_j$ are reconstructed, the Lagrange
polynomials
\[
  p_j(t)=\prod_{k\ne j}\frac{t-\lambda_k}{\lambda_j-\lambda_k}
\]
give the actual loaded source vectors
\begin{equation}
  \boxed{z_j=p_j(\mathscr K)Z=e_jZ.}
  \label{eq:store-source-reconstruction-master}
\end{equation}
For any admitted future functionals $f_1,\ldots,f_m$, the matrix
$R_{ij}=f_i(z_j)$ separates all loaded frequency sources exactly when
$R^*R\succ0$.
\end{corollary}

\begin{proof}
The rectangular Vandermonde factorization
$\mathbb H_r=V_rWV_r^{\mathsf T}$ has rank $\min\{r+1,s\}$.  Functional
calculus gives $p_j(\mathscr K)=e_j$ on the finite loaded spectrum.  Finally,
for a coefficient vector $c$,
\[
  c^*R^*Rc
  =\sum_i\left|f_i\left(\sum_jc_jz_j\right)\right|^2,
\]
so positivity is exactly finite future separation on the loaded span.
\end{proof}

\begin{assessment}[No sharp central-frequency Read is consumed]
\label{assess:store-no-PVM-master}
The vectors $z_j$ are finite polynomials in the physical Store jet applied to
the retained pointer source.  The reconstruction uses scalar moments and
future comparisons; it does not assume that the spectral projections $e_j$
are separately admitted sharp outcomes.  Spectral loading and physical future
visibility remain distinct finite tests.
\end{assessment}


\subsection{Analytic Store-germ polar clock}

\begin{theorem}[Analytic Store germ generates the polar clock]
\label{thm:analytic-store-polar-master}
Assume \Cref{ass:predictive-clock-entry}\textup{(S3)--(S4)}.  On the positive
Store support let
\[
  M=(-L^2)^{1/2}=\sum_{j=1}^{r}\mu_je_j,
  \qquad
  J=LM^{-1},
  \qquad 0<\mu_1<\cdots<\mu_r.
\]
Then the physical finite-word control group contains the blockwise product
\[
  \boxed{\mathcal Q_{\rm pol}=\operatorname{Ad}(e^{\pi J/4}).}
\]
On every positive block it is the frequency-independent quarter-root.  No
central-frequency measurement, branch-conditioned dwell, or frequency
commensurability is required.  If a zero block $e_0$ is present, the theorem
acts as the identity there and leaves that block as a separate real
countersector.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Let $H(u)$ be the analytic Hamiltonian germ.  On the $j$-th positive block,
blockwise irreducibility implies that some Taylor coefficient has a nonzero
matrix element between the two eigenspaces of $L_j$.  The corresponding
analytic commutator is not identically zero.  Since only finitely many loaded
blocks occur, one control value $u_*$ can be chosen outside the finite union of
its zero sets, so the skew generator $B$ of $H(u_*)$ is transverse to $L$ on
every positive block.

Put $D=\operatorname{ad}_L$ and let $R_j$ be the transverse derivation on block
$j$.  The operator
\[
  \mathscr T(Y)=-\frac14[D,[D,Y]]
\]
satisfies $\mathscr T(R_j)=\mu_j^2R_j$.  The Lagrange polynomial
\[
  p_j(s)=\prod_{k\ne j}\frac{s-\mu_k^2}{\mu_j^2-\mu_k^2}
\]
therefore gives $R_j=p_j(\mathscr T)R$.  Each $R_j$, its commutator with $D$,
and their mutual commutator span the full blockwise $\mathfrak{su}(2)$.  The
finite direct sum of these Lie algebras is generated by the original physical
one-parameter controls.  The algebraic subgroup they generate is open in the
connected product $\prod_j\mathrm{PU}(2)_j$ and hence equals that product.
It contains the blockwise quarter rotations
$\operatorname{Ad}(e^{\pi J_j/4})$; their product is
$\operatorname{Ad}(e^{\pi J/4})$.  Since $L$ vanishes on $e_0$, the same
commutator construction produces no second transverse axis there.
\end{proof}

\begin{corollary}[Real score pulse produces the polar microtick]
\label{cor:real-score-polar-master}
Let $Y_J=iJ$ and suppose the physical control group contains $R$ with
$RZR^*=Y_J$.  For any Hermitian metric-score operator $X(q)$,
\[
 \boxed{
 (R\otimes I)e^{-iZ\otimes X(q)/\sqrt N}(R^*\otimes I)
 =e^{J\otimes X(q)/\sqrt N}.}
\]
Thus the complex polar microtick is a derived conjugate of one real
pointer--score interaction.
\end{corollary}

\begin{proof}
Conjugation carries the generator $-iZ\otimes X(q)$ to
$-iY_J\otimes X(q)=J\otimes X(q)$, and functional calculus preserves the
exponential.
\end{proof}

\subsection{Finite semantic-score depth and common-clock certification}
\label{sec:semantic-clock-certification-master}

Retain the reconstructed Store data
$\mathscr K=\sum_j\lambda_je_j$, $z_j=e_jZ$, and
$w_j=\norm{z_j}^2$.  Let $A(q)$ be the unique positive Radon--Nikodym
coordinate of the successful one-step metric-score refinement on the retained
source-cyclic carrier, so $0\preceq A(q)\preceq I$.

\begin{proposition}[One-step score effects do not determine score depth]
\label{prop:score-effect-depth-no-go-master}
There are two finite instruments with the same one-step effect and therefore
the same one-step success probability on every state, but with different
two-step success probabilities.
\end{proposition}

\begin{proof}
On $\C^2$ take $A=|0\rangle\langle0|$.  The Lueders success map
$\mathcal I_{\rm L}(\rho)=A\rho A$ and the measure-and-prepare success map
\[
  \mathcal I_{\rm P}(\rho)
  =\Tr(A\rho)|1\rangle\langle1|
\]
have the same effect because both have success probability $\Tr(A\rho)$.
Starting from $|0\rangle\langle0|$, both first steps succeed with probability
one.  The Lueders instrument succeeds again with probability one, whereas the
prepare-$|1\rangle$ instrument succeeds with probability zero.
\end{proof}

\begin{definition}[Persistent positive score-transfer certificate]
\label{def:persistent-score-transfer-master}
A controlled score refinement has a persistent positive score transfer when,
after one fixed retained identification of its source-cyclic record, the
physical probability of $n$ consecutive successful score steps from a retained
source vector $z$ is
\begin{equation}
  \boxed{p_n(q;z)=\langle z,A(q)^nz\rangle.}
  \label{eq:persistent-score-depth-master}
\end{equation}
This is a finite product/no-memory certificate for the instrument update.  It
is not a consequence of one-step Radon--Nikodym effect access.
\end{definition}

All subsequent powers of $A(q)$ are used only after this certificate passes.
A failure is retained as an explicit score-memory or state-update source.

\begin{theorem}[Exact score-frequency mixing identity]
\label{thm:score-frequency-mixing-master}
For every self-adjoint $A$ on the finite Store source carrier,
\begin{equation}
  \boxed{
  \norm{[\mathscr K,A]}_{\HS}^2
  =\sum_{j,k=1}^{s}(\lambda_j-\lambda_k)^2
    \norm{e_jAe_k}_{\HS}^2.}
  \label{eq:score-mixing-HS-master}
\end{equation}
In particular,
\begin{equation}
  \boxed{[\mathscr K,A]=0
  \iff A=\sum_je_jAe_j.}
  \label{eq:score-mixing-zero-master}
\end{equation}
For a partition $\mathfrak P$ of the spectral labels, put
$E_C=\sum_{j\in C}e_j$ and assume
\[
  \Delta_{\mathfrak P}
  :=\min_{C\ne D}\min_{j\in C,k\in D}
  |\lambda_j-\lambda_k|>0.
\]
Then
\begin{equation}
  \boxed{
  \norm{A-\sum_{C\in\mathfrak P}E_CAE_C}_{\HS}
  \le
  \frac{\norm{[\mathscr K,A]}_{\HS}}{\Delta_{\mathfrak P}}.}
  \label{eq:score-cluster-leakage-master}
\end{equation}
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Insert $I=\sum_je_j$ on both sides of $A$.  Since
\[
  e_j[\mathscr K,A]e_k
  =(\lambda_j-\lambda_k)e_jAe_k
\]
and the matrix blocks are Hilbert--Schmidt orthogonal, summation proves
\eqref{eq:score-mixing-HS-master}.  Distinctness of the $\lambda_j$ gives the
zero criterion.  In the cluster complement every surviving block joins two
different clusters and carries a spectral factor of magnitude at least
$\Delta_{\mathfrak P}$, proving the leakage estimate.
\end{proof}

Assume henceforth that $[\mathscr K,A(q)]=0$ and write
$A_j(q)=e_jA(q)e_j$.  For $r,n\ge0$ define
\begin{align}
  m_{r,n}(q)&:=\langle Z,\mathscr K^rA(q)^nZ\rangle,
  \label{eq:mixed-score-moment-master}\\
  p_{j,n}(q)&:=\langle z_j,A_j(q)^nz_j\rangle,
  \qquad p_{j,0}=w_j.
  \label{eq:block-score-moment-master}
\end{align}

\begin{theorem}[Finite mixed Store--score reconstruction]
\label{thm:mixed-score-reconstruction-master}
Let $V_{rj}=\lambda_j^r$ for $0\le r\le s-1$.  For every fixed score depth
$n$,
\begin{equation}
  \boxed{
  \begin{pmatrix}m_{0,n}\\ \vdots\\ m_{s-1,n}\end{pmatrix}
  =V
  \begin{pmatrix}p_{1,n}\\ \vdots\\ p_{s,n}\end{pmatrix},
  \qquad
  (p_{j,n})_{j=1}^{s}=V^{-1}(m_{r,n})_{r=0}^{s-1}.}
  \label{eq:mixed-score-Vandermonde-master}
\end{equation}
Thus finitely many mixed histories reconstruct the semantic success sequence
loaded by every Store-frequency source without a sharp central-frequency Read.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Commutation gives
$\mathscr K^rA^nZ=\sum_j\lambda_j^rA_j^nz_j$.  Orthogonality of the spectral
blocks yields
$m_{r,n}=\sum_j\lambda_j^rp_{j,n}$.  The Vandermonde determinant is nonzero.
\end{proof}

Define the first blockwise Cauchy--Schwarz defect
\begin{equation}
  \Delta_j^{\rm sem}(q)
  :=w_jp_{j,2}(q)-p_{j,1}(q)^2\ge0.
  \label{eq:semantic-block-defect-master}
\end{equation}

\begin{theorem}[Depth-two common source-clock theorem]
\label{thm:semantic-clock-depth-two-master}
Assume $[\mathscr K,A(q)]=0$.  The following are equivalent.
\begin{enumerate}[label=\textup{(\roman*)},leftmargin=2.8em]
\item There is $a(q)\in[0,1]$ such that
      \begin{equation}
        \boxed{A_j(q)z_j=a(q)z_j\qquad(1\le j\le s).}
        \label{eq:semantic-common-eigen-master}
      \end{equation}
\item Every $\Delta_j^{\rm sem}(q)$ vanishes and the ratios
      $a_j(q)=p_{j,1}(q)/w_j$ are independent of $j$.
\item For every $j,n\ge0$,
      \begin{equation}
        \boxed{p_{j,n}(q)=w_ja(q)^n.}
        \label{eq:semantic-common-moments-master}
      \end{equation}
\item With $p_n(q)=\langle Z,A(q)^nZ\rangle$,
      \begin{equation}
        \boxed{p_0(q)p_2(q)=p_1(q)^2.}
        \label{eq:semantic-global-depth-two-master}
      \end{equation}
\end{enumerate}
Hence, after the frequency-mixing and persistent-transfer tests, one- and
two-depth score successes prove the exact all-depth common source clock.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
For one block, Cauchy--Schwarz applied to $z_j$ and $A_jz_j$ gives
$p_{j,1}^2\le w_jp_{j,2}$, with equality exactly when
$A_jz_j=a_jz_j$, where $a_j=p_{j,1}/w_j$.  This proves the equivalence of the
first two statements.  The eigenvector identity gives all powers, proving the
third and its converse at depths one and two.

Globally, Cauchy--Schwarz gives $p_1^2\le p_0p_2$, with equality exactly when
$A(q)Z=a(q)Z$.  Since $A$ commutes with every $e_j$, applying $e_j$ gives
$A_jz_j=a(q)z_j$ for every loaded block.  The converse is immediate.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Exact total-variance clock decomposition]
\label{thm:semantic-total-variance-master}
Fix a metric tangent $h$ and assume the centered even expansion
\begin{equation}
  A(th)=I-\frac{t^2}{2}Q_h+t^4B_h+o(t^4),
  \qquad Q_h=Q_h^*\succeq0,
  \qquad [Q_h,\mathscr K]=0.
  \label{eq:semantic-score-expansion-master}
\end{equation}
Put $w_*=\sum_jw_j$ and
$\omega(X)=\langle Z,XZ\rangle/w_*$.  Define
\[
  \nu_j(h)=\frac{\langle z_j,Q_hz_j\rangle}{w_j},
  \qquad
  \operatorname{Var}_j(Q_h)
  =\frac{\langle z_j,Q_h^2z_j\rangle}{w_j}-\nu_j(h)^2.
\]
For
\begin{equation}
  \mathfrak D_{\rm clk}(th)
  :=\log\frac{p_0(th)p_2(th)}{p_1(th)^2},
  \label{eq:semantic-clock-defect-master}
\end{equation}
one has
\begin{equation}
  \boxed{
  \left.\frac{\dd^4}{\dd t^4}\right|_{t=0}
  \mathfrak D_{\rm clk}(th)
  =6\operatorname{Var}_{\omega}(Q_h).}
  \label{eq:semantic-fourth-variance-master}
\end{equation}
Moreover,
\begin{equation}
  \boxed{
  \operatorname{Var}_{\omega}(Q_h)
  =\frac1{w_*}\sum_jw_j\operatorname{Var}_j(Q_h)
   +\frac1{w_*}\sum_jw_j
    \bigl(\nu_j(h)-\bar\nu(h)\bigr)^2,}
  \label{eq:semantic-total-variance-master}
\end{equation}
where $\bar\nu(h)=w_*^{-1}\sum_jw_j\nu_j(h)$.  The first term is
intra-frequency semantic multiplicity; the second is an inter-frequency
semantic-loading split.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
The expansion gives
\begin{align*}
  p_1(th)/w_*
  &=1-\frac{t^2}{2}\omega(Q_h)+t^4\omega(B_h)+o(t^4),\\
  p_2(th)/w_*
  &=1-t^2\omega(Q_h)
    +t^4\left[\frac14\omega(Q_h^2)+2\omega(B_h)\right]+o(t^4).
\end{align*}
The unknown fourth-order term cancels from $p_0p_2/p_1^2$, leaving
\[
  \mathfrak D_{\rm clk}(th)
  =\frac{t^4}{4}
   \left[\omega(Q_h^2)-\omega(Q_h)^2\right]+o(t^4).
\]
Multiplication by $4!$ proves the first formula.  Since $Q_h$ is block
diagonal,
\[
  \omega(Q_h^2)
  =\frac1{w_*}\sum_jw_j
   \left[\operatorname{Var}_j(Q_h)+\nu_j(h)^2\right].
\]
Subtracting the square of the weighted mean gives the law of total variance.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Centrally unmarked semantic alternative]
\label{thm:no-unmarked-semantic-split-master}
Assume that the loaded Store-frequency sources are separated by the mixed
Store--score window, that the score family is primitive-natural, and that the
active phase contains no future-consequential natural central response label.
If every block is source-rank one,
$A(q)z_j=a_j(q)z_j$, then
\begin{equation}
  \boxed{a_1(q)=\cdots=a_s(q)}
  \label{eq:no-unmarked-semantic-split-master}
\end{equation}
throughout the tested control neighbourhood.  Thus either the exact common
clock holds, or the unequal loading is an explicit marked central semantic
sector; there is no future-relevant but unmarked third case.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
If the $a_j(q)$ are unequal, then
$C(q)=\sum_ja_j(q)e_j$ is a non-scalar natural central positive contraction.
The one-step mixed moments separate its values on the loaded sources, so it is
future consequential.  This contradicts central unmarkedness.  Equality of the
amplitudes and the rank-one condition give
\Cref{thm:semantic-clock-depth-two-master}.
\end{proof}

\begin{firewall}[Semantic tomography does not assume the central PVM]
The mixed moments are matrix coefficients of finite words in the Store jet and
the admitted score-success transfer.  They algebraically recover blockwise
semantic data.  They do not by themselves assert that the sharp projections
$e_j$, or the spectral projections of $A(q)$, are admitted physical effects.
\end{firewall}


\begin{firewall}[Finite membership versus uniform control resources]
\Cref{thm:analytic-store-polar-master} is an exact finite-cutoff statement.
A regulator-uniform clock additionally requires a positive control-frame Gram,
a bounded-resource target word, or a cluster-adapted Store-frequency compiler.
Concrete sufficient certificates are given in
\Cref{thm:control-frame-equivalence-master,thm:quantitative-control-word-master,thm:optimal-transverse-pulse-master,cor:polar-Gram-resource-master,thm:cluster-Bernstein-filter-master}.
Collapse of those quantitative certificates is retained as a control-resource
obstruction; it is not absorbed into a phase convention.
\end{firewall}

\subsection{Anchored-return realization retained as a sufficient special case}


\begin{theorem}[Anchor-return rigidity]
\label{thm:anchor-return-rigidity-master}
Let $U(t)=e^{-itG}$ be any retained anchored reversible orbit with unit anchor
$\Omega$, return probability $p(t)=|\langle\Omega,U(t)\Omega\rangle|^2$,
and assume $p''(0)<0$ and $p^{(4)}(0)=2p''(0)^2$.  Put
\[
 c=\langle\Omega,G\Omega\rangle,
 \qquad H=G-cI,
 \qquad \mu_n=\langle\Omega,H^n\Omega\rangle.
\]
Then
\[
 p''(0)=-2\mu_2,
 \qquad
 p^{(4)}(0)=2\mu_4+6\mu_2^2,
\]
and
\[
 \boxed{
 \frac12p^{(4)}(0)-p''(0)^2
 =\mu_4-\mu_2^2
 =\|(H^2-\mu_2I)\Omega\|^2\ge0.}
\]
The displayed binary return identity is equivalent to
\[
 H^2\Omega=\nu^2\Omega,
 \qquad
 \nu^2=-\frac12p''(0)>0.
\]
Consequently the minimal source-cyclic carrier is
\[
 W=\Span\left\{\Omega,\nu^{-1}H\Omega\right\}\cong\C^2,
\]
and, in that orthonormal basis,
\[
 H|_W=\nu
 \begin{pmatrix}0&1\\1&0\end{pmatrix},
 \qquad
 \boxed{p(t)=\cos^2(\nu t).}
\]
The anchored projective microtick on $W$ is unique up to the unique
source-fixing unitary and an irrelevant scalar phase.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Projective centering gives
$U(t)=e^{-ict}e^{-itH}$ and $\mu_1=0$.  Writing
$\phi(t)=\langle\Omega,e^{-itH}\Omega\rangle$,
\[
 \phi(t)=1-\frac{\mu_2}{2}t^2
 +\frac{i\mu_3}{6}t^3+\frac{\mu_4}{24}t^4+O(t^5).
\]
Since $p(t)=\phi(t)\phi(-t)$,
\[
 p(t)=1-\mu_2t^2
 +\left(\frac{\mu_4}{12}+\frac{\mu_2^2}{4}\right)t^4
 +O(t^6),
\]
which proves the derivative formulas.  The norm identity follows by expanding
$\langle(H^2-\mu_2I)^2\rangle$.  Hence
$p^{(4)}(0)=2p''(0)^2$ is equivalent to
$H^2\Omega=\mu_2\Omega$.  Set $e_0=\Omega$ and
$e_1=\nu^{-1}H\Omega$.  They are orthonormal, and
$He_0=\nu e_1$, $He_1=\nu e_0$, proving the matrix formula and cyclic
minimality.  Its spectral values are $\pm\nu$ with anchor weights $1/2$, so
the return amplitude is $\cos(\nu t)$.  The canonical basis also proves the
source-fixing uniqueness statement.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Quarter-root, transverse Read, and complex clock factor]
\label{thm:clock-quarter-root-master}
On $W$ define
\[
 X=\nu^{-1}H|_W,
 \qquad
 Z=2|\Omega\rangle\langle\Omega|-I_W,
 \qquad
 t_*=\frac{\pi}{4\nu},
\]
and let
\[
 \mathcal Q=\Ad(e^{-it_*H}),
 \qquad
 Y=-\mathcal Q(Z).
\]
Then $X,Y,Z$ are pairwise anticommuting Hermitian involutions.  Moreover,
\[
 \mathcal Q(X)=X,
 \qquad
 \mathcal Q^2(Z)=-Z,
 \qquad
 \Ad(Z)\mathcal Q\Ad(Z)=\mathcal Q^{-1}.
\]
Conjugating the anchor Read by $\mathcal Q$ gives the physical sharp
$Y$-Read, and
\[
 \boxed{C^*_{\C}(P_0,U(t):t\in\R)=M_2(\C).}
\]
The half-period lift $-iX$ is anti-Hermitian and has square $-I_W$.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
In the clock basis,
\[
 X=\begin{pmatrix}0&1\\1&0\end{pmatrix},
 \qquad
 Z=\begin{pmatrix}1&0\\0&-1\end{pmatrix},
 \qquad
 e^{-it_*H}=e^{-i\pi X/4}.
\]
Direct multiplication gives
$e^{-i\pi X/4}Ze^{i\pi X/4}=-Y$ with
$Y=\begin{psmallmatrix}0&-i\\i&0\end{psmallmatrix}$.
The Pauli relations prove all involution, anticommutation, and dihedral
identities.  A conjugate of a sharp admitted Read by a physical reversible
process is a sharp admitted Read.  Since $P_0=(I+Z)/2$ and the orbit contains
$X$, the generated complex algebra is all of $M_2(\C)$.  At time
$\pi/(2\nu)$, the centered unitary is $-iX$.
\end{proof}

\begin{corollary}[Exact fair clock transition]
\label{cor:fair-clock-transition-master}
Every $Y$-eigenstate is unbiased for the anchor $Z$-Read:
\[
 \Pr(\pm_Z\mid\pm_Y)=\frac12.
\]
The canonical Store--Read versus Read--Store Hankel window therefore has
\[
 \boxed{\det\mathsf H_{\rm ord}=\frac12,
 \qquad \eta=\frac13.}
\]
\end{corollary}

\begin{proof}
Anticommutation gives
$P^Y_sP^Z_tP^Y_s=\frac12P^Y_s$.  The two displayed constants are the direct
$2\times2$ order-window calculation with transition probability $q=1/2$.
\end{proof}

\subsection{Depth-two external Clifford factor and physical direction Read}

\begin{theorem}[One-anchor depth-two external Clifford construction]
\label{thm:anchor-external-clifford-master}
Take two locally addressable clock copies $A,B$ and define
\[
 \boxed{
 \begin{aligned}
 \gamma^0&=-iX_A\otimes I_B,\\
 \gamma^1&=Z_A\otimes Z_B,\\
 \gamma^2&=Z_A\otimes Y_B,\\
 \gamma^3&=Y_A\otimes I_B.
 \end{aligned}}
\]
Then
\[
 (\gamma^0)^2=-I,
 \qquad
 (\gamma^i)^2=I,
 \qquad
 \gamma^\mu\gamma^\nu+\gamma^\nu\gamma^\mu=0
 \quad(\mu\ne\nu),
\]
and
\[
 \boxed{C^*_{\C}(\gamma^0,\gamma^1,\gamma^2,\gamma^3)
 =M_4(\C)\cong\Cl_{1,3}(\C).}
\]
The effects of $\gamma^1$ are implemented by local $Z/Z$ Reads and parity;
those of $\gamma^2$ by local $Z/Y$ Reads and parity; those of $\gamma^3$ by
the local $Y$-Read.  Choosing the three procedures with probability $1/3$
gives six outcomes $\pm e_i$ satisfying, for every state $\rho$,
\[
 \boxed{
 \sum_{i=1}^3\sum_{s=\pm1}
 p_{i,s}(\rho)(se_i)(se_i)^{\mathsf T}=\frac13I_3.}
\]
After the canonical external replacement/twirl, the local parity branches are
completion-equivalent to the corresponding Lueders parity branches.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Every mixed anticommutator contains exactly one local Pauli anticommutation,
which proves the Clifford relations.  The generators give
$X_A\otimes I$ and $Y_A\otimes I$, hence $Z_A\otimes I$; multiplying by
$\gamma^1$ and $\gamma^2$ gives $I\otimes Z_B$ and $I\otimes Y_B$, hence
$I\otimes X_B$.  Therefore both full local matrix factors are generated.
For Hermitian involutions $A,D$,
\[
 \sum_{ad=s}P^A_a\otimes P^D_d=\frac12(I+sA\otimes D),
\]
which proves the parity-effect claims.  For one fixed axis the two sign effects
sum to $I/3$, so their second-moment contribution is
$e_ie_i^{\mathsf T}/3$.  Summing the three axes gives $I_3/3$.  Complete
external replacement discards within-eigenspace coherence, so two branch
instruments with the same effects have identical completed outputs.
\end{proof}

\subsection{Complete renewal cuts and derived fresh copies}

\begin{theorem}[Rank-one complete cut is exactly a replacement]
\label{thm:rank-one-cut-replacement-master}
Let
$\mathcal R:\mathcal T(\Hh_-)\to\mathcal T(\Hh_+)$ be the completed reset on
the complete physical cut span, and let admitted futures separate its reachable
output range.  The complete cut response matrix has rank one if and only if
there is one normalized state $\omega$ such that
\[
 \boxed{\mathcal R(A)=\Tr(A)\omega.}
\]
If the rank is greater than one, an admitted future distinguishes two reset
outputs and therefore constructs an explicit consequential memory source.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Future tomography identifies the response-matrix rank with the dimension of the
linear output span.  If that span is one dimensional, write
$\mathcal R(A)=\lambda(A)\omega$ with $\Tr\omega=1$.  Trace preservation gives
$\lambda(A)=\Tr A$.  The converse is immediate.  If the output span has
dimension at least two, future separation provides an admitted effect with
different values on two reachable outputs.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Minimal replacement dilation and past--fresh split]
\label{thm:replacement-dilation-master}
Let $\omega$ have rank $r$, choose a purification
$|\Omega_\omega\rangle\in\Hh_+\otimes\mathcal E_\omega$, and let
$J:\Hh_-\to\mathcal E_{\rm mem}$ be unitary.  Then
\[
 \boxed{V_{\rm rep}|\psi\rangle
 =|\Omega_\omega\rangle\otimes J|\psi\rangle}
\]
is a minimal Stinespring isometry of the replacement channel, with environment
dimension $r\dim\Hh_-$.  For every reference system $R$,
\[
 \boxed{(I_R\otimes V_{\rm rep})|\Psi\rangle
 \cong (I_R\otimes J)|\Psi\rangle\otimes|\Omega_\omega\rangle.}
\]
Thus the past is transferred to one memory factor while a fixed fresh factor is
prepared.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Tracing the two environment factors gives $\Tr(A)\omega$.  The Choi matrix is
$I_{\Hh_-}\otimes\omega$, whose rank is $r\dim\Hh_-$, equal to the displayed
environment dimension; hence the dilation is minimal.  The reference-system
identity follows by expanding $|\Psi\rangle$ in the input factor and reordering
tensor factors.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Complete renewal-cut factorization]
\label{thm:complete-cut-factorization-master}
Under \Cref{ass:complete-cut-entry}, every natural post-cut control has the
form
\[
 W_y=I_{\rm past}\otimes V_y,
\]
and the complete polarized source factors as
\[
 \boxed{\Xi(x,y)=\xi_{\rm past}(x)\otimes\xi_{\rm fut}(y).}
\]
Consequently
\[
 \boxed{
 K((x,y),(x',y'))
 =K((x,0),(x',0))K((0,y),(0,y')).}
\]
Slot transitivity transports this identity to every one-slot cut and yields an
exact identical serial root.  If the loaded past action is reducible, all
natural residual memory lies in its isotypic labels and multiplicity spaces;
those sectors must be retained explicitly rather than called fresh.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
By \Cref{thm:rank-one-cut-replacement-master,thm:replacement-dilation-master},
the cut has the canonical past--fresh split.  Let $U_g$ be the loaded past
action on the selected unresolved sector.  By
\Cref{ass:complete-cut-entry}\textup{(F3)}, it is irreducible, so
$U(G)'=\C I$ by Schur's lemma and
\[
 \{U_g\otimes I:g\in G\}'=I\otimes\mathcal B(\mathcal F).
\]
Naturality places every $W_y$ in this commutant, proving
$W_y=I\otimes V_y$.  Applying it to
$\xi_{\rm past}(x)\otimes\Omega_{\rm fresh}$ gives the source product and
hence the kernel product.  Slot transitivity gives the same rank-one
flattening at each slot; iterating the factorization gives the identical serial
root.  In the reducible decomposition
$\bigoplus_\pi\mathcal V_\pi\otimes\mathcal M_\pi$, the commutant is
$\bigoplus_\pi I_{\mathcal V_\pi}\otimes\mathcal B(\mathcal M_\pi)$, which
locates every possible natural memory exactly.
\end{proof}

\begin{corollary}[Resolved-memory conditional factorization]
\label{cor:resolved-memory-factorization-master}
If the loaded past action is reducible and its consequential isotypic and
multiplicity memory is physically resolved into orthogonal source-minimal
sectors labelled by $z$, then on every resolved sector with irreducible
remaining past action,
\begin{equation}
  \boxed{
  K_z((x,y),(x',y'))
  =K_z^{\rm past}(x,x')K_z^{\rm fut}(y,y').}
  \label{eq:resolved-memory-factorization-master}
\end{equation}
The unconditional kernel is a finite direct sum, or a classical mixture after
discarding a sharp label.  Without physical resolution, a multiplicity space
remains a quantum source factor and no classical conditional-product statement
is asserted.
\end{corollary}

\begin{proof}
The physical record decomposes the past carrier into orthogonal source-minimal
sectors.  Apply \Cref{thm:complete-cut-factorization-master} on each sector with
irreducible remaining action.  Orthogonality gives the direct sum, and
discarding a sharp classical label gives the corresponding convex mixture.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Approximate Schur locality at a renewal cut]
\label{thm:approximate-cut-locality-master}
Let $U:G\to\mathcal U(\mathcal P)$ be an irreducible unitary representation of
a finite or compact group and let $W$ be a unitary on
$\mathcal P\otimes\mathcal F$.  Define
\[
  \varepsilon_{\rm nat}(W)
  :=\sup_{g\in G}
  \norm{W(U_g\otimes I)-(U_g\otimes I)W}.
\]
If $\varepsilon:=\varepsilon_{\rm nat}(W)<1/2$, there is a unitary
$V\in\mathcal U(\mathcal F)$ such that
\begin{equation}
  \boxed{
  \norm{W-I_{\mathcal P}\otimes V}
  \le
  \eta_{\rm nat}(\varepsilon)
  :=\varepsilon+1-\sqrt{1-2\varepsilon}.}
  \label{eq:approximate-cut-locality-master}
\end{equation}
In particular, $\eta_{\rm nat}(\varepsilon)=2\varepsilon+O(\varepsilon^2)$.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Average over the group:
\[
  \overline W
  :=\int_G(U_g^*\otimes I)W(U_g\otimes I)\,\dd g.
\]
Then $\norm{W-\overline W}\le\varepsilon$.  The average lies in the
commutant of $U(G)\otimes I$, so irreducibility and Schur's lemma give
$\overline W=I_{\mathcal P}\otimes A$ for a contraction $A$ on $\mathcal F$.
Since $W$ is unitary,
\[
  \norm{A^*A-I}
  =\norm{\overline W^*\overline W-W^*W}
  \le2\varepsilon.
\]
Thus $A$ is invertible and its least singular value is at least
$\sqrt{1-2\varepsilon}$.  Its polar unitary
$V=A(A^*A)^{-1/2}$ satisfies
\[
  \norm{A-V}=\norm{|A|-I}
  \le1-\sqrt{1-2\varepsilon}.
\]
The triangle inequality proves the result.
\end{proof}

\begin{corollary}[Approximate fresh-source and product-kernel bound]
\label{cor:approximate-cut-product-master}
Assume an exact complete replacement split at the cut and suppose every
normalized post-cut control has naturality defect at most
$\varepsilon<1/2$.  There is a product source family
$\widetilde\Xi(x,y)=\xi_{\rm past}(x)\otimes
\widetilde\xi_{\rm fut}(y)$ such that
\begin{equation}
  \boxed{\norm{\Xi(x,y)-\widetilde\Xi(x,y)}
  \le\eta_{\rm nat}(\varepsilon),}
  \label{eq:approximate-source-product-master}
\end{equation}
and
\begin{equation}
  \boxed{
  \left|
  K((x,y),(x',y'))
  -K_{\rm past}(x,x')K_{\rm fut}(y,y')
  \right|
  \le2\eta_{\rm nat}(\varepsilon).}
  \label{eq:approximate-kernel-product-master}
\end{equation}
\end{corollary}

\begin{proof}
Apply \Cref{thm:approximate-cut-locality-master} to each post-cut control and
put $\widetilde\xi_{\rm fut}(y)=V_y\Omega_{\rm fresh}$.  The vector estimate is
the operator-norm estimate on a unit source.  For unit vectors $a,b$ and
approximants $\widetilde a,\widetilde b$,
\[
  |\langle a,b\rangle-\langle\widetilde a,\widetilde b\rangle|
  \le\norm{a-\widetilde a}+\norm{b-\widetilde b}.
\]
\end{proof}

\begin{definition}[Scalar positive source short]
\label{def:scalar-source-short-master}
For $c\ne0$ and a second source vector $o$, define
\begin{equation}
  \mathfrak S_{o\mid c}
  :=\norm{o}^2-
  \frac{|\langle c,o\rangle|^2}{\norm c^2}
  =\norm{(I-P_c)o}^2,
  \label{eq:scalar-source-short-master}
\end{equation}
where $P_c$ projects onto $\C c$.
\end{definition}

\begin{theorem}[A rank-one renewal cut cannot create a source short]
\label{thm:cut-source-short-master}
Let clock and geometry source vectors pass through the same exact renewal cut
and the same post-cut factor:
\[
  c=c_-\otimes f,
  \qquad
  o=o_-\otimes f.
\]
Then
\begin{equation}
  \boxed{
  \mathfrak S_{o\mid c}
  =\norm f^2\mathfrak S_{o_-\mid c_-}.}
  \label{eq:cut-source-short-master}
\end{equation}
In particular, the source short vanishes after the cut exactly when it vanished
before the cut.  A positive clock--geometry short must therefore be localized
in a pre-cut source multiplicity or an explicit consequential memory sector;
it cannot be blamed on the common fresh factor.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Use
\[
  \norm c^2=\norm{c_-}^2\norm f^2,
  \qquad
  \norm o^2=\norm{o_-}^2\norm f^2,
  \qquad
  |\langle c,o\rangle|^2
  =|\langle c_-,o_-\rangle|^2\norm f^4
\]
in \eqref{eq:scalar-source-short-master}.
\end{proof}


\begin{corollary}[Derived common-source freshness diamond]
\label{cor:derived-fresh-diamond-master}
Under \Cref{ass:complete-cut-entry}, two regenerated external legs conditioned
on the complete source label $z$ have kernel
\[
 \boxed{
 K_\diamond((z,a,b),(z',a',b'))
 =P(z,z')R_{z'}(a')R_{z'}(b').}
\]
Its stationary law is exchange invariant, so every bounded exchange-odd
observable has zero stationary mean.  This is a freshness and permutation
statement only.  It neither proves metric compatibility nor removes torsion;
those are decided by \Cref{thm:physical-connection-master}.
\end{corollary}

\begin{proof}
The complete cut theorem gives independent identical fresh factors after
conditioning on every consequential source/memory label.  Composing the source
transition with the two identical Read laws gives the displayed kernel and
stationary law $\pi(z)R_z(a)R_z(b)$.  Exchange-odd expectations vanish by
pairing $(a,b)$ with $(b,a)$.  The kernel contains no frame-link or plaquette
information, so no connection conclusion follows.
\end{proof}

\subsection{Integrated finite closure and the obsolete superschemas}

\begin{theorem}[Predictive Store--control finite closure]
\label{thm:predictive-clock-discharge}
\label{thm:two-principle-discharge}
Every bare datum satisfying \Cref{ass:predictive-clock-entry} has, on its
selected positive Store-active recurrent component:
\begin{enumerate}[label=\textup{(\roman*)},leftmargin=2.7em]
\item the canonical predictive Radon--Nikodym envelope and automatic
      naturality of the minimal Store realization and canonical refinements;
\item exact finite Store-jet reconstruction of every loaded positive frequency,
      source weight, and annihilating recurrence;
\item a physical frequency-independent polar quarter-root generated by finite
      Store/control words;
\item a sharp transverse Read and a future-distinct exchange-odd history line;
\item a negative-square temporal generator and a marked local $M_2(\C)$ factor;
\item at tensor depth two, a marked
      $M_4(\C)\cong\Cl_{1,3}(\C)$ external factor and the six-direction Read;
\item the completely predictive external replacement/twirl reset.
\end{enumerate}
Full matrix-effect completeness on every finite power, arbitrary environment
POVMs, a separately imposed PAN schema, Store-frequency primitivity, and the
anchored fourth-order return identity are not premises of this theorem.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
The predictive envelope and naturality are
\Cref{thm:predictive-RN-master,thm:minimal-realization-naturality-master}.
The Store spectrum and source weights are
\Cref{thm:store-autocorrelation-master,thm:store-frequency-pencil-master,cor:store-frequency-count-master}.
The polar clock is \Cref{thm:analytic-store-polar-master}; the real score
compiler is \Cref{cor:real-score-polar-master}.  The loaded odd line is
converted to an exchange-covariant Read by
\Cref{thm:consequential-odd-read-master}.  The external Clifford factor and
six-direction instrument are \Cref{thm:anchor-external-clifford-master}; its
proof consumes only a physical qubit clock and therefore applies to the
Store-derived polar clock.  Uniform averaging of the generated finite Pauli
action gives the canonical twirl.
\end{proof}

\begin{corollary}[Reduced-hypothesis local Lorentzian cell]
\label{cor:reduced-hypothesis-spacetime}
Finite repetitions of the cell in
\Cref{thm:predictive-clock-discharge} generate the accumulated external record
group $\Z^3$, a unique positive renewal pressure on the selected branching
support, and the Lorentzian principal symbol
\[
 \mathscr D(\xi)^2=(-\xi_0^2+|\vec\xi|^2)I_4.
\]
Under the flat analytic scaling package, the discrete Hamiltonians converge in
strong-resolvent sense to the massless $(3+1)$ Dirac Hamiltonian.  A curved
Levi--Civita conclusion additionally requires the physical link, metricity,
torsion, face-composition, and scaling tests of
\Cref{thm:physical-connection-master}; freshness alone is not sufficient.
\end{corollary}

\begin{proof}
The finite cell is \Cref{thm:predictive-clock-discharge}.  Signed accumulation
of the three external direction records gives $\Z^3$.  Strong connectivity,
positive finite depths, and the noncycle condition give the unique pressure
root.  The flat convergence is the retained common-core theorem.  The final
sentence is exactly the connection alternative proved in
\Cref{thm:physical-connection-master}.
\end{proof}

\begin{principle}[Legacy recurrent process-complete nonclassicality (RPCN)]
\label{prin:RPCN}
The historical RPCN superschema assumes complete complex matrix state/effect
spaces on every finite power, minimal pure reversible dilations, retention of
every minimal environment, and admission of every finite environment POVM.
It is retained only for comparison with earlier versions and is not a premise
of \Cref{thm:layered-programme-master}.
\end{principle}

\begin{principle}[Legacy primitive automorphism naturality (PAN)]
\label{prin:PAN}
The historical PAN superschema separately imposed covariance of the Store,
every resolved Read, and the reset under primitive automorphisms.  For the
canonical objects used here, this covariance is instead
\Cref{thm:minimal-realization-naturality-master} and the uniqueness of their
invariant characterization.
\end{principle}

\begin{assessment}[Exact hypothesis reduction]
RPCN and PAN remain sufficient historical envelopes when a model is already
known to satisfy them, but they are no longer the flagship entrance.  PAN has
been removed as an independent principle for canonical Store--Read objects.
The strongest RPCN access clause has been replaced by complete future
separation of the loaded source lines.  All-power closure has been replaced by
ordinary locally addressable tensor depth two.  Fresh-copy independence has
been replaced by the rank and commutant tests of
\Cref{ass:complete-cut-entry}; it is not inferred from identical marginals or
from stationarity alone.
\end{assessment}

\begin{remark}[Structural time versus a selected arrow]
The determinant line, its two-sheeted cover, and $(\gamma^0)^2=-I$ are
structural conclusions even in a deck-symmetric stationary state.  A nonzero
expectation of a deck-odd observable, and hence one realized temporal
orientation, remains boundary, superselection, or collective state data.
\end{remark}



% =============================================================================
\section{The single-writer Clifford realization of the metric-volume source}
\label{sec:single-writer-clifford}
% =============================================================================

The exterior determinant source can be represented without adjoining an
independent exterior-state carrier.  Let the marked external factor contain
Hermitian spatial generators
\[
 (\gamma^i)^*=\gamma^i,
 \qquad
 \gamma^i\gamma^j+\gamma^j\gamma^i=2\delta^{ij}I_4,
 \qquad 1\le i,j\le3,
\]
and put
\[
 \gamma(u)=\sum_{i=1}^3u_i\gamma^i,
 \qquad
 \omega_3=\gamma^1\gamma^2\gamma^3,
 \qquad
 \mathcal J_3=i\omega_3,
 \qquad
 \tau_4(A)=\frac14\Tr A.
\]
Then $\mathcal J_3$ is a Hermitian involution and $\Tr\mathcal J_3=0$.

\begin{theorem}[Single-writer Clifford source and common-trace criterion]
\label{thm:single-writer-clifford-master}
Let $Q\cong\R^3$ be the oriented external response space and let
\[
 W:Q\longrightarrow
 \Span_{\R}\{\gamma^1,\gamma^2,\gamma^3\}
\]
be real linear.  There is a unique $L_W\in M_3(\R)$ such that
$W(v)=\gamma(L_Wv)$.  Define
\[
 G_W(u,v)=\tau_4(W(u)W(v)),
 \qquad
 \chi_W=i\tau_4\!\left(
 \mathcal J_3W(e_1)W(e_2)W(e_3)\right).
\]
Then:
\begin{enumerate}[label=\textup{(\roman*)},leftmargin=2.7em]
\item the exterior top line is already the Clifford pseudoscalar line:
      \[
       \operatorname{Alt}_3(\gamma(u),\gamma(v),\gamma(w))
       =\det[u\ v\ w]\,\omega_3,
      \]
      and
      \[
       i\tau_4\!\left(
       \mathcal J_3\gamma(u)\gamma(v)\gamma(w)\right)
       =\det[u\ v\ w];
      \]
\item the common trace and signed determinant loading obey
      \[
       \boxed{G_W=L_W^{\mathsf T}L_W,\qquad
       \chi_W=\det L_W,\qquad |\chi_W|^2=\det G_W;}
      \]
\item for every real coframe-control matrix $E$,
      \[
       \boxed{
       i\tau_4\!\left(
       \mathcal J_3W(Ee_1)W(Ee_2)W(Ee_3)\right)
       =\chi_W\det E;}
      \]
\item the following are equivalent:
      \[
       \chi_W\ne0,
       \qquad G_W\succ0,
       \qquad \rank W=3;
      \]
\item if $W$ is equivariant for the orientation-preserving icosahedral group,
      in the sense that
      \[
       W(gv)=S_gW(v)S_g^*,
       \qquad S_g\gamma(v)S_g^*=\gamma(gv),
      \]
      then for one real scalar $\lambda$,
      \[
       \boxed{W(v)=\lambda\gamma(v),\qquad
       G_W=\lambda^2I_3,\qquad \chi_W=\lambda^3.}
      \]
\end{enumerate}
Thus one finite quadratic trace tensor determines the magnitude of the cubic
metric-volume source.  No auxiliary copy of $\Lambda^\bullet Q$ and no three
independently normalized spatial writers are required.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
The Clifford relations give
$\tau_4(\gamma(u)\gamma(v))=u\cdot v$.  The map
\[
 (u,v,w)\longmapsto
 i\tau_4(\mathcal J_3\gamma(u)\gamma(v)\gamma(w))
\]
is alternating: after interchanging two arguments, the anticommutator terms
have Clifford degree one after multiplication by $\mathcal J_3$ and hence zero
trace.  It is therefore a scalar multiple of the oriented volume form.  At
$(e_1,e_2,e_3)$ one has
$\mathcal J_3\gamma^1\gamma^2\gamma^3=-iI_4$, so the scalar is one.  The same
alternating argument with values in $M_4(\C)$ gives the displayed Chevalley
identity and identifies $\Lambda^3Q$ with $\C\omega_3\subset M_4(\C)$.

Writing $W(v)=\gamma(L_Wv)$ gives
\[
 G_W(u,v)=(L_Wu)\cdot(L_Wv)
 =u^{\mathsf T}L_W^{\mathsf T}L_Wv,
\]
so $G_W=L_W^{\mathsf T}L_W$.  Applying the determinant trace identity to the
three columns of $L_W$ gives $\chi_W=\det L_W$; taking determinants of the Gram
identity gives $|\chi_W|^2=\det G_W$.  Replacing the columns of $L_W$ by those
of $L_WE$ proves the coframe formula.  Invertibility of $L_W$ is equivalent to
positive definiteness of its Gram matrix and to nonvanishing determinant.
Finally, under icosahedral equivariance, $L_W$ commutes with the irreducible
three-dimensional icosahedral representation.  Schur's lemma makes its complex
extension scalar, and reality makes that scalar real.  The remaining formulas
follow from the preceding identities.
\end{proof}

\begin{corollary}[Exact third-Store-jet determinant and quantitative loading]
\label{cor:single-writer-third-jet-master}
For $E\in M_3(\R)$ define
\[
 U_E(t)=
 e^{-itW(Ee_1)}e^{-itW(Ee_2)}e^{-itW(Ee_3)},
 \qquad
 a_E(t)=\tau_4(\mathcal J_3U_E(t)).
\]
Then
\[
 a_E(0)=a_E'(0)=a_E''(0)=0,
 \qquad
 \boxed{\frac1{6}a_E'''(0)=\chi_W\det E.}
\]
Moreover, if $G_W\succeq g_*I_3$ with $g_*>0$, then
\[
 \boxed{|\chi_W|\ge g_*^{3/2}.}
\]
Hence a single sequential three-control Store history writes the determinant
at its first nonzero pseudoscalar order, and a uniform one-writer frame bound
is already a quantitative nonvanishing certificate for that source.
\end{corollary}

\begin{proof}
Expand the three exponentials through order three.  Terms of total degree zero,
one, or two have zero $\mathcal J_3$-trace.  At degree three, terms containing
three powers from fewer than three distinct factors reduce by
$W(v)^2=|L_Wv|^2I_4$ to degree-one terms and again have zero trace.  The only
surviving coefficient is
\[
 (-i)^3\tau_4\!\left(
 \mathcal J_3W(Ee_1)W(Ee_2)W(Ee_3)\right)
 =\chi_W\det E,
\]
which is the coefficient of $t^3$ and proves the derivative identity.  The
eigenvalues of $G_W$ are the squared singular values of $L_W$; their product is
$|\chi_W|^2$, giving the lower bound.
\end{proof}

\begin{firewall}[Logical status of the single-writer reduction]
The predictive Store--control entrance provides the marked external Clifford
factor and its physical frame Reads through
\Cref{thm:anchor-external-clifford-master}.  The theorem above does not by
itself prove that an uncontrolled microscopic Store has a nondegenerate
grade-one jet or that the pseudoscalar quadrature is retained.  It proves that
once those finite process tests pass, the determinant writer is already
internal to the existing external factor and has only one common normalization
tensor.
\end{firewall}

% =============================================================================
\section{Canonical metric histories and exterior-source geometrodynamics}
\label{sec:exterior-geometrodynamics}
% =============================================================================

\begin{theorem}[Exterior-cube determinant identity]
\label{thm:exterior-cube-master}
Let $u_1,\ldots,u_m\in\R^3$ and $w_a>0$, $\sum_aw_a=1$.  Put
\[
 M=\sum_aw_au_au_a^{\mathsf T}\succ0,
 \qquad
 We_a=\sqrt{w_a}\,u_a.
\]
Then
\[
 \boxed{
 \norm{\bigwedge^3W}_{\HS}^2
 =\sum_{a<b<c}w_aw_bw_c\det[u_a,u_b,u_c]^2
 =\det M.}
\]
For three independent direction records $U_1,U_2,U_3$ with this law,
\[
 \boxed{\mathbb E\det[U_1,U_2,U_3]^2=6\det M.}
\]
In particular, the canonical isotropic record $M=I_3/3$ has ordered
squared-volume mean $2/9$.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
The matrix coefficients of $\bigwedge^3W$ are the $3\times3$ minors
$\sqrt{w_aw_bw_c}\det[u_a,u_b,u_c]$.  Cauchy--Binet identifies their squared
sum with $\det(WW^*)=\det M$.  The ordered expectation counts each unordered
triple $3!$ times, while repeated labels have zero determinant.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Spectral-source determinant Ward theorem]
\label{thm:spectral-source-Ward-master}
Fix $q_0\in\operatorname{Sym}_3^+$, a unit source $e_0$ in a finite retained
environment, and define
\[
 v(q)=\left(\frac{\det q}{\det q_0}\right)^{1/2}e_0.
\]
For every fixed positive operator $F\succeq0$ let
$\mathscr R_F(q)=\langle v(q),Fv(q)\rangle$.  Then
\[
 \boxed{
 \mathscr R_F(q)=c_F\frac{\det q}{\det q_0},
 \qquad c_F=\langle e_0,Fe_0\rangle\ge0.}
\]
If $c_F>0$, the normalized negative configuration Hessian is
\[
 \boxed{
 \mathfrak B_q(h,k)
 =\Tr(q^{-1}h\,q^{-1}k)
  -\Tr(q^{-1}h)\Tr(q^{-1}k).}
\]
It has one negative trace direction and five positive traceless directions.
Its cotangent inverse is
\[
 \boxed{
 \mathfrak B_q^{-1}(P,Q)
 =\Tr(PQ)-\frac12\Tr(P)\Tr(Q).}
\]
No assumption on the dimension of the source-cyclic space is required; all
additional cyclic modes enter only through $c_F$.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
The scalar factor in $v(q)$ leaves the quadratic form, giving the determinant
response.  For $X=q^{-1}h$ and $Y=q^{-1}k$,
\[
 D^2\det(q)[h,k]
 =\det(q)\bigl(\Tr X\Tr Y-\Tr(XY)\bigr),
\]
which gives $\mathfrak B_q$.  Decompose
$X=X_0+xI$ with $\Tr X_0=0$.  Then
$\mathfrak B_q(X,X)=\Tr(X_0^2)-6x^2$, proving signature $(5,1)$.
On the traceless subspace the form is the trace pairing; on the scalar line
it multiplies by $-2$ relative to the trace pairing.  Inverting these two
blocks gives $\Tr(PQ)-\tfrac12\Tr(P)\Tr(Q)$.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Exact scalar-response anomaly]
\label{thm:scalar-response-anomaly-master}
Let $F(q)\succ0$ be $C^2$, put
$c(q)=\langle e_0,F(q)e_0\rangle$, $\ell=\log c$, and
$\theta_q(h)=\Tr(q^{-1}h)$.  For
$\mathscr R(q)=(\det q/\det q_0)c(q)$, the departure from the pure determinant
Ward tensor is
\[
 \boxed{
 \begin{aligned}
 \mathcal W_q(h,k)={}&-D^2\ell_q[h,k]
 -D\ell_q[h]D\ell_q[k]\\
 &-\theta_q(h)D\ell_q[k]-\theta_q(k)D\ell_q[h].
 \end{aligned}}
\]
Thus $D\ell=0$ is sufficient for exact ADM balance.  If
$F(q)=U(q)F(q_0)U(q)^*$ and $U(q)e_0=e^{i\alpha(q)}e_0$, then $c(q)$ is
constant and the anomaly vanishes.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Differentiate $(\det q)c(q)$ twice and subtract the normalized determinant
Hessian.  The identity $c^{-1}D^2c=D^2\ell+D\ell\otimes D\ell$ yields the
formula.  The conjugacy hypothesis fixes the source expectation of $F$.
\end{proof}

\begin{definition}[Exterior-source normality]
An uncontrolled microscopic normal-history sector has exterior-source
normality when, after future-null shorting and canonical frame
identification, its positive response has the form
\[
 \mathscr R(q)=\langle v(q),Fv(q)\rangle
\]
with the determinant loading above and one frame-independent $F\succeq0$.
This is a sufficient condition for the \emph{uncontrolled} generator; it is
not an additional premise for the canonical response-core completion.
\end{definition}

\begin{theorem}[Canonical exterior-source DeWitt completion]
\label{thm:canonical-ADM-master}
Inside the predictive anchored-clock class, the canonical three-fresh-Read
response core has a nonzero exterior source, a physically lifted frame action,
and a frame-covariant completed return preserving the minimal response cube.
Consequently:
\begin{enumerate}[label=\textup{(\roman*)},leftmargin=2.7em]
\item its determinant-character line is one dimensional inside the coherent
      response cube;
\item ancillary determinant-character copies, if present in a larger retained
      pointer or future environment, change only the scalar stiffness whenever
      they are fed by the same volume loading;
\item the canonical local normal kinetic tensor is proportional to
      \[
        \Tr(\Pi^2)-\frac12(\Tr\Pi)^2;
      \]
\item the DeWitt trace coefficient $1/2$ is fixed independently of the overall
      microscopic action coefficient.
\end{enumerate}
For a separately chosen uncontrolled microscopic normal generator, the same
DeWitt conclusion is conditional on exterior-source normality, with every
frame-dependent response failure measured by
\Cref{thm:scalar-response-anomaly-master}.  The reciprocal normalization of the
kinetic and curvature terms, and therefore the full normal--normal ADM bracket,
is an additional common-action statement audited in
\Cref{thm:ADM-action-audit-master,thm:Clifford-common-action-master}.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
The external response cube and its physical Reads are supplied by
\Cref{thm:predictive-clock-discharge,thm:anchor-external-clifford-master}.
\Cref{thm:minimal-realization-naturality-master} lifts the physical frame action
uniquely, while \Cref{thm:complete-cut-factorization-master} supplies the three
fresh response legs whenever the full completed-return claim is made.  The top
exterior power of the embedded three-dimensional response transforms by the
determinant character.  Multiplicity one inside the coherent response cube and
Schur's lemma make every covariant return scalar on this line.  Positive
functional calculus, Feshbach correction, or a higher Krylov space fed by the
same vector therefore produces one fixed positive $F$ acting on the determinant
loading.  Apply \Cref{thm:spectral-source-Ward-master}; inversion of the
determinant Hessian in three dimensions gives the coefficient $1/(3-1)=1/2$.
None of these steps identifies the coefficient multiplying the determinant
Hessian with the one multiplying the curvature Read, so the final normalization
claim is correctly separated and delegated to the inserted-action theorem.
\end{proof}

% =============================================================================
\section{Exact flagship source, chronology, and ADM action audit}
\label{sec:flagship-source-chronology}
% =============================================================================
